Calling IIT Kharagpur … ?  All   five - digit   telephone numbers listed in this Directory are our internal (EPABX).   All  numbers   starting with   50, 60, 80,   81, 82 and 83 can be accessed by Direct Inward  Dialing (DID) facility by dialing 03222 2xxxxx (withi n India) or +91 3222 2xxxxx (from  outside India), where xxxxx are five digit numbers listed in this book.   For   DID to numbers  starting with 84 & 85   remove 8 and prefix   21 . For   example,   84335 can be called as  03222 - 21 4335   Other numbers can be accessed throug h the Auto Atte ndent facility at  03222   –   281005 .  Six digit numbers listed in this book are of direct BSNL telephones.   Additional direct  telephones are available at  Director’s Res.   277201  Director’s Office   255386  Dy. Institute   is   getting   In ternet   services   through   20   Gbps   link   of   National   Knowledge  Network. Further details are available at: http://www.cic.iitkgp.ac.in.  Email address in this directory are given as username@domainname or username@.  Please append “iitkgp.ac.in” like office@adm. iitkgp.ac.in   and for the second type like  contact.iic@iitkgp.ac.in. Facilities available at IIT Kharagpur EP A BX  Call Transfer   :   You are in conversation with   A . To transfer the call to   B , make a hookflash and  dial   B   without replacing the receiver. After   B   replies, drop down the receiver. In  case of getting   B   engaged or no   reply, make a hook flash to come back to   A .  Call split   :   Transfer the call as above. When you are in line with   B   keeping   A   in hold  position, dial   2   to come back to   A   keeping   B   in hold. Dialing repeatedly, you can  toggle between   A   and   B.  Conference   :   Mak e call transfer to   B . When you are in line with   B   keeping   A   in hold position,  dial   3   for 3 - party conferencing.  Call back   :   When you get some number busy/no answer, dial   6   get a tone, and replace the  receiver. You will get automatic ring back as soon as th e desired line becomes  free.  Call Forward   :   You can forward your telephone number to some other telephone by dialing   93  followed by the destination telephone number followed by   # . You can cancel the  same by dialing   94 .  STD Locking   :   Subscribers with STD   facilities can lock the facility by dialing   97   followed by a  four - digit code followed by   # .   To unlock dial   96   followed by the same four - digit  code followed by   # .   To change the code dial   98   followed by your old code  followed   by *   followed by new code follow ed by   # .  Call pick up   :   If this facility is registered among some subscribers, any one can pick up others  call by dialing   91.
